{"forms":[{"form":"insides","tags":["plural"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"noun","senses":[{"examples":[{"text":"The inside of the building has been extensively restored.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["The interior or inner part."]},{"examples":[{"text":"On a motorway, you should never pass another vehicle on the inside.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["The left-hand side of a road if one drives on the left, or right-hand side if one drives on the right."]},{"examples":[{"text":"The car in front drifted wide on the bend, so I darted up the inside to take the lead.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["The side of a curved road, racetrack etc. that has the shorter arc length; the side of a racetrack nearer the interior of the course or some other point of reference."]},{"examples":[{"text":"Eating that stuff will damage your insides.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["The interior organs of the body, especially the guts."],"tags":["colloquial","in-plural"]},{"glosses":["A passenger within a coach or carriage, as distinguished from one upon the outside."],"tags":["UK","colloquial","dated"]},{"glosses":["The inside scoop; information known only to certain involved people."],"tags":["slang"]}],"sounds":[{"ipa":"/ˈɪnsaɪd/"},{"ipa":"/ˌɪnˈsaɪd/"}],"upstream_line":176934,"upstream_offset":835524023,"upstream_record_sha256":"668f6e9c551634f56d774b26e166e5a39d7f8fe7cdbdcbe763baf942fd7f36c9","word":"inside"}
{"antonyms":[{"word":"outside"}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"adj","senses":[{"examples":[{"text":"The inside surface of the cup is unpainted.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["Of or pertaining to the inner surface, limit or boundary."],"tags":["not-comparable"]},{"examples":[{"text":"Because of the tighter bend, it's harder to run in an inside lane.","type":"example"},{"text":"All the window seats were occupied, so she took an inside seat.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["Nearer to the interior or centre of something."],"tags":["not-comparable"]},{"examples":[{"text":"The reporter had received inside information about the forthcoming takeover.","type":"example"},{"text":"The robbery was planned by the security guard: it was an inside job.","type":"example"},{"text":"They wanted to know the inside story behind the celebrity's fall from grace.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["Originating from, arranged by, or being someone inside an organisation."],"tags":["not-comparable"]},{"antonyms":[{"word":"outside"}],"glosses":["Legally married to or related to (e.g. born in wedlock to), and/or residing with, a specified other person (parent, child, or partner); (of a marriage, relationship, etc) existing between two such people."],"raw_tags":["of a person"],"tags":["not-comparable"]},{"examples":[{"text":"The first pitch is ... just a bit inside.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["Toward the batter as it crosses home plate."],"raw_tags":["of a pitch"],"tags":["not-comparable"],"topics":["ball-games","baseball","games","hobbies","lifestyle","sports"]},{"examples":[{"text":"the inside lane of the motorway","type":"example"}],"glosses":["At or towards or the left-hand side of the road if one drives on the left, or right-hand side if one drives on the right."],"tags":["not-comparable"]}],"sounds":[{"ipa":"/ˈɪnsaɪd/"},{"ipa":"/ˌɪnˈsaɪd/"}],"upstream_line":176935,"upstream_offset":835548662,"upstream_record_sha256":"a02456951a7dbd9df44c991b55df5e41228dfe90799bea7c3d943d2908c45fe9","word":"inside"}
{"lang_code":"en","pos":"adv","senses":[{"examples":[{"text":"I opened the book and found a hidden note inside.","type":"example"},{"text":"It started raining, so I went inside.","type":"example"},{"text":"The secretive residents of the massive city-ship tended to stay inside.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["Within or towards the interior of something; within the scope or limits of a place, especially a building."],"tags":["not-comparable"]},{"examples":[{"text":"It was snowing, so the children stayed inside.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["Within or towards the interior of something; within the scope or limits of a place, especially a building.","Indoors."],"tags":["not-comparable"]},{"examples":[{"text":"I cleaned the window inside.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["Within or towards the interior of something; within the scope or limits of a place, especially a building.","On the inner side of something."],"tags":["not-comparable"]},{"examples":[{"text":"He spent ten years inside, doing a stretch for burglary.","type":"example"},{"text":"She will have to go inside unless the jury sides with her.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["Within or towards the interior of something; within the scope or limits of a place, especially a building.","In or to prison."],"tags":["colloquial","not-comparable"]},{"examples":[{"text":"That's something I truly believe inside.","type":"example"},{"text":"He knows the truth, but he's keeping it inside.","type":"example"},{"text":"Look inside to find the strength you need to go on.","type":"example"},{"text":"Are you laughing at us inside?","type":"example"}],"glosses":["Intimately, secretly or in one's inner heart or soul; without expressing what one is feeling or thinking."],"tags":["not-comparable"]}],"sounds":[{"ipa":"/ˈɪnsaɪd/"},{"ipa":"/ˌɪnˈsaɪd/"}],"upstream_line":176936,"upstream_offset":835567964,"upstream_record_sha256":"cb33c3a5182de8820998d025173c94ede5dca798d8318b97885e5b0cfbbc4223","word":"inside"}
{"lang_code":"en","pos":"prep","senses":[{"examples":[{"text":"He placed the letter inside the envelope.","type":"example"},{"text":"He was inside the doorway.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["Within the interior of, closest to the center or to a specific point of reference; on the interior side of."]},{"examples":[{"text":"She walked inside the house.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["To the interior of."]},{"examples":[{"text":"The job was finished inside two weeks.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["Within (a period of time)."]}],"sounds":[{"ipa":"/ˈɪnsaɪd/"},{"ipa":"/ˌɪnˈsaɪd/"}],"upstream_line":176937,"upstream_offset":835599317,"upstream_record_sha256":"680625a69470697d74f956594b0d12ad8cb232cb24b85e05a82ad6209374fc2a","word":"inside"}
